- **money-flow**: Money Flow Index (volume-weighted RSI) — the conviction check. Use when the user asks "does this move have volume behind it?", "is this rally real or thin?", "MFI on &lt;ticker&gt;", "is there accumulation/distribution?", or as part of analyzing the daily brief to separate volume-backed moves from thin drifts.
